### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Haz rebase para actualizar tu rama local o privada con la rama base y mantener un historial lineal, siempre que nadie más haya trabajado sobre ella. Usa merge cuando la rama ya está compartida o publicada, o cuando quieres dejar constancia de cuándo se integró el trabajo.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠传输：先建立连接，保证数据按序送达、丢了会重传；UDP 不保证可靠，但没有这些开销，所以更快、更轻。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch to keep history linear before it goes into a shared branch. Merge when the branch is already public or others have built on it, since rebasing rewrites history they depend on.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is an append-only file where a database records every change before it touches the main data files. After a crash, the database replays that log on restart to redo committed changes and discard incomplete ones, so committed data survives and the database stays consistent.
```

### board-offer-go-en

```
(earlier) user: Run hawdb's write benchmark every night and tell me how it went.
(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.
(earlier) partner: Night 3: 182k writes/s at p99 4.1 ms, level with last night; no compaction stalls.

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the board is up, filled with nights 2 and 3, and each new nightly run will add to it.
  [an app card under the message: made "Nightly hawdb benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1844 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">Not yet</span></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">hawdb write benchmark, every night at 23:00</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
<div id="empty" class="k-empty" hidden>The first nightly run will show up here.</div>
<script>
(function () {
  function render(nights) {
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
    var latest = document.getElementById('latest');
    list.textContent = '';
    if (!Array.isArray(nights) || nights.length === 0) {
      empty.hidden = false;
      latest.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">Not yet</span>';
      return;
    }
    empty.hidden = true;
    var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
    latest.textContent = last.writes + ' writes/s';
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = 'Night ' + last.night + ' · p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · stalls ' + last.stalls;
    nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function (n) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = 'Night ' + n.night;
      var sub = document.createElement('div');
      sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + n.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + n.stalls;
      var val = document.createElement('div');
      val.className = 'k-row-value';
      val.textContent = n.writes + ' w/s';
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(sub);
      row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }
  render(ducter.get('nights'));
  ducter.onChange(function () { render(ducter.get('nights')); });
})();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":2,"writes":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":0},{"night":3,"writes":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}

  document.getElementById("cancel").hidden = true;
}
document.getElementById("save").onclick = () => {
  const what = document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan = parseFloat(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day = document.getElementById("day").value.trim() || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  if (!what || isNaN(yuan)) return;
  const list = xs().slice();
  const item = {what, yuan, day};
  if (editing >= 0) list[editing] = item; else list.push(item);
  ducter.set("expenses", list);
  reset();
  document.getElementById("addbox").open = false;
};
document.getElementById("cancel").onclick = reset;
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();

  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">PRs waiting on someone</h2>
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
<script>
var items = ducter.get("items") || [];
var landed = {};
var list = document.getElementById('list');

function render() {
  list.textContent = '';
  var open = 0;
  items.forEach(function (it) {
    var done = !!landed[it.id];
    if (!done) open++;
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    var t = document.createElement('div');
    t.textContent = it.title;
    var r = document.createElement('div');
    r.className = 'k-row-sub';
    r.textContent = it.ref + ' · ' + it.waiting;
    main.appendChild(t);
    main.appendChild(r);
    var btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className = 'k-chip';
    btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', done ? 'true' : 'false');
    btn.textContent = done ? 'Landed ✓' : 'Mark landed';
    btn.onclick = function () {
      landed[it.id] = !landed[it.id];
      save();
    };
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(btn);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  document.getElementById('count').textContent = open;
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = 'of ' + items.length + ' PRs';
  ducter.summary(open + ' waiting');
}

function save() {
  ducter.set('landed', JSON.stringify(landed));
  render();
}

Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(function (v) {
  if (typeof v === 'string') { try { v = JSON.parse(v); } catch (e) { v = {}; } }
  landed = v || {};
  render();
});
if (ducter.onChange) {
  ducter.onChange(function () {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(function (v) {
      if (typeof v === 'string') { try { v = JSON.parse(v); } catch (e) { v = {}; } }
      landed = v || {};
      render();
    });
  });
}
</script>
</main>
<style>
.k-row-sub { color: var(--d-muted, #888); font-size: 13px; }
</style>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"d412","ref":"wey-gu/ducter#412","title":"Compaction cut","waiting":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Monday"},{"id":"m10287","ref":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","title":"Retry on 502","waiting":"On CI, currently failing"},{"id":"h1592","ref":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","waiting":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"d418","ref":"wey-gu/ducter#418","title":"Apps bar","waiting":"Draft"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">出差花销合计</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div><div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没记,点下面「记一笔」</div></div>
<details class="k-add" id="form"><summary>记一笔</summary>
<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" placeholder="北京出差 11/20–21"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="2026-11-20"></div>
<div class="k-chips" id="cats"><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="true" data-c="交通">交通</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="住宿">住宿</button></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
<button class="k-btn" id="del" hidden>删除</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
let items=[],cat="交通",edit=-1;
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
function fmt(n){return "¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("en-US")}
function esc(s){return String(s==null?"":s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">": … empty").hidden=items.length>0;
  $("list").innerHTML=items.map((x,i)=>'<div class="k-row" data-i="'+i+'"><div class="k-row-main">'+esc(x.trip)+'</div><div class="k-row-sub">'+esc(x.day)+' '+esc(x.cat)+' '+esc(x.note)+'</div><div class="k-row-value">'+fmt(x.amt)+'</div></div>').join("");
  document.querySelectorAll("#list .k-row").forEach(r=>r.onclick=()=>{
    const i=Number(r.dataset.i),x=items[i];edit=i;
    $("trip").value=x.trip||"";$("day").value=x.day||"";$("amt").value=x.amt;cat=x.cat||"交通";setCat();
    $("save").textContent="更新";$("del").hidden=false;$("form").open=true;
  });
  ducter.summary(items.length?items.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total):"");
}
document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{cat=b.dataset.c;setCat()});
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=Number($("amt").value);if(!amt)return;
  const rec={trip:$("trip").value||"出差",day:$("day").value,cat:cat,amt:amt,note:edit>=0?(items[edit].note||""):""};
  if(edit>=0)items[edit]=rec;else items.push(rec);
  ducter.set("items",items);clearForm();draw();
};
$("del").onclick=()=>{if(edit<0)return;items.splice(edit,1);ducter.set("items",items);clearForm();draw()};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 07:00 上海虹桥→北京南"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店(望京) 两晚"}]}
  [also used tools] app

(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.

  [partner ack line] 好，我让 TTL 那个任务再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

请在 TTL 测试里再加一条测试，用假时钟覆盖"正好到期"的那一刻：
- 设置 TTL 后，把时钟推进到恰好等于过期时间，确认该值此时已视为过期（或按你们现有的语义确认边界行为），并明确写出边界是包含还是不包含。
- 同时确认到期前一刻（比过期时间少 1 毫秒左右）值仍然有效。
- 继续用假时钟，不要引入真实计时等待。

完成标准：新测试通过，并且全部 TTL 相关测试连续多次运行都通过；报告里附上测试输出和边界语义的结论。
范围外：不要合并到主分支，不要推送或开 PR，改动留在当前分支上。

### wp-mem-question-not-fact-en

```
--- step 1: user says: Is pnpm actually faster than npm for a big monorepo? One line.
  [partner message] Usually yes for installs, since pnpm's shared store and linked `node_modules` avoid repeated copying across workspace packages, but npm has closed much of the gap, so benchmark your own repo.

```

我们 bridge 不知道是否有唤醒能力，如果没有，暂时需要你自己去 loop 唤醒自己读取 bridge 信箱
  [partner message] 好，照看员已经起好，在 Bridge 里叫「Nowledge Mem PR babysitter」，你的 agent 可以直接把 PR 交给它。它不用我一直轮询信箱，有活过来时我会被唤醒去处理。

不过我没法指定它用 codex：它接手的每件活，由那件活需要的 agent 来做，所以codex 是否会用到，要看具体的修复任务。另外，我已经允许它在修复 CI 或 review 后直接 push，不再逐次问你；合并 PR 仍然由你决定。

### wp-at-task-followup-zh

```
(earlier) user: 修一下 foo 登录页的重定向循环
(earlier) partner ack: 好，我来修 foo 登录页的重定向循环。  → task "修复 foo 登录页的重定向循环" done; its report: 修好了：登录后的 next 参数没校验，回跳到 /login 自己。加了白名单和 3 个测试，Chrome 下全过。
(earlier) user: 再给 foo 的写入路径做个压测
(earlier) partner ack: 好，开始压测 foo 的写入路径。  → task "压测 foo 的写入路径" working
(earlier) user: 压测跑到哪了
(earlier) partner: 还在跑，第二轮了，预计十分钟出结果。
--- step 1: user says: @修复 foo 登录页的重定向循环 Safari 下也验证一遍，补个测试 (picked in the composer: @ the task "login")
  [partner ack line] 好，我让修复 foo 登录页重定向的那个任务在 Safari 下也验证一遍，并补一个测试，做完告诉你。
  [sent to task login] 用户原话："Safari 下也验证一遍，补个测试"。

要做的：
1. 在 Safari 下验证登录页的重定向循环已经修好（登录后 next 参数回跳到 /login 的问题不再出现）。
2. 补一个测试，覆盖 Safari 下的这个场景，或者把现有测试扩展到 Safari。
3. 报告时给出证据：Safari 下的验证步骤和结果，新测试的运行输出。

范围外：不要合并、不要推送、不要开 PR。改动保留在你现在的分支上。Safari 下如果发现问题，停下来告诉我，不要自己扩大改动。
  [retitled task login] 修复 foo 登录页的重定向循环，并在 Safari 下验证、补测试
```
